    synopsis:
      eyebrow: 'W. A. Mozart · Singspiel in two acts · Act I available'
      paragraphs:
        0: 'The young prince Tamino is pursued by a serpent and rescued by the Queen of the Night’s three attendants. She shows him a portrait of Pamina, who is being held in Sarastro’s domain, and persuades him to set out to rescue her. The bird-catcher Papageno joins him, carrying magic bells, while Tamino receives a magic flute.'
        1: 'Papageno finds Pamina and they try to escape together. Tamino reaches the temple of wisdom, where he begins to doubt what he has been told about Sarastro. At the end of Act I, Pamina and Tamino meet in Sarastro’s presence, and the two young men are ordered to enter the temple for their trials.'

                      A villain escaped with her.
                      I still see her trembling
                      With fearful shocks,
                      Your fearful quake
                      Your shy life.
                      I had to see her robbing me,
                      Oh help! was all she said:
                      Their plea was in vain,
                      Because my help was too weak.
                      You will leave them free,
                      You will be the daughter's savior.
                      And I will see you as the winner,
                      Then be yours forever.
